# Verified capabilities registry

Answers exactly one question: **what does the system actually do, and what proves it** — kept
separate from `decisions/`, because an accepted architectural decision means only an approved
direction, not working code (see `FrameworkDoc.md`, section 7). Rows are added only in a
documentation commit made by the human (gate 3) — the Developer and QA roles **propose** rows in
their reports, ready to paste; they do not write here directly.

Evidence column uses exactly one of four values:

- **mutation-checked test** — a test exists, and a mutation was run against the mechanism it
  guards, and recorded in the mutation log below.
- **test, no mutation** — a test exists and is green, but no mutation has been run against it yet.
  This is weaker proof than it looks — see `FrameworkDoc.md`, section 6.
- **manual verification, `<date>`** — checked by hand once; will go stale silently if nothing
  re-checks it.
- **no evidence** — documented and possibly implemented, but nothing here proves it works. This is
  the honest default for a new capability row, not a flaw in the registry.

## Capabilities

| Capability | Requirement ref | Evidence | Reference |
|---|---|---|---|
| Draft assumption reset preview skips the scenario override and resolves target margin and overload threshold from project then live organization; any organization-sourced preview value requires `ORGANIZATION_DEFAULTS_READ`, reset is denied unless the entire preview is authorized, and the organization-default source is locked through the guarded reset write | F-02, ADR-0005/0007/0012, Issue #270 K-01-K-09 | mutation-checked test | `backend/tests/test_scenario_assumption_reset_preview.py` K-01-K-09; [PR #272](https://github.com/TanerCRB/StafffingCalculator/pull/272) |
| Project creation submits the six F-01 fields through the Projects screen; validates and selects the server result; enforces `PROJECT_CREATE`; retries an unresolved create with the same caller/key/payload; rejects changed-payload and cross-caller replays; rechecks authorization before replay; and reconciles off-page creates with the server list without inflating its rows or total | F-01, Issue #228 K-01–K-07, ADR-0005, ADR-0009 addendum 2026-10-04 | mutation-checked test | `frontend/src/features/projects/ProjectCreateForm.test.tsx` (K-01–K-05, R-02–R-04, off-page reconciliation and visible replay); `backend/tests/test_project_create_idempotency.py` (K-06/K-07 replay, changed payload, caller scope, authorization re-check and concurrency); `backend/tests/test_project_create_required_fields.py`; `backend/tests/test_access_control.py`; [PR #248](https://github.com/TanerCRB/StafffingCalculator/pull/248); mutation log below. |
| Project Copy submits one request for the selected visible row; validates and selects the server response with its draft scenarios; reconciles list filters and pagination from the server; an off-page copy remains selected in the details panel and is found by manual pagination; permission and named unsupported-model refusals create no phantom row | F-01, Issue #231 K-01–K-06, SC-1-03 | mutation-checked test | `frontend/src/features/projects/ProjectListScreen.test.tsx` (`shows the validated copy and selected scenarios after reconciling the server list`, `copies the project whose row action was selected`, `clears an Archived filter and refreshes from the server when copying an archived project`, `keeps copied details selected and lets the user page to a copy with a repeated name`, `does not add a phantom row when copy permission is denied`, `explains the named unsupported-model refusal without adding a phantom row`); [PR #246](https://github.com/TanerCRB/StafffingCalculator/pull/246); mutation log below. This is frontend behavior evidence; backend copy semantics remain evidenced by SC-1-03 tests. |
| The Projects screen lets a PM archive an active project after an explicit irreversible-action confirmation; success and uncertain outcomes reconcile from the server, definite refusal preserves the visible status, and pending submission cannot be duplicated | F-01, Issue #232 K-01–K-04 | mutation-checked test | `frontend/src/features/projects/ProjectListScreen.test.tsx` (`archives an active project and keeps it listed as Archived`, `retains Active status when archive request is refused`, `does not send duplicate archive requests while one is pending`, `explains archive cannot be undone before submission`, `checks the refreshed list when an archive request returns 5xx`); [PR #250](https://github.com/TanerCRB/StafffingCalculator/pull/250); mutation log below. UI tests do not re-prove backend authorization or persistence. |

| Strażnik wyścigu `/results`, `/compare` i what-if (SC-7-03) — **uściśla wiersze strażnika wyżej (SC-7-01: "przez porównanie `assumptions_used.rate_source`"; SC-6-04 what-if; SC-4-03: "porównuje tylko źródła zależne od statusu")**: jedna funkcja `refuse_a_status_race`, wołana po trzecim odczycie, na statusach zamrożonych osobno przez każdy z trzech odczytów odświeżających scenariusz (przychód, koszt osobowy, koszt dodatkowy — `status_at_read`; nigdy `.status` współdzielonego obiektu `Scenario`, nigdy `rate_source` przychodu porównany z kosztowym). `409` ⇔ (a) przychód zależny od statusu (`rate_source` ∈ `STATUS_DEPENDENT_SOURCES`, także odpowiedź bez reguły) i rozjazd przychód↔koszt, albo (b) rozjazd koszt↔koszt dodatkowy dla każdego modelu (luka R-01 SC-7-03, obecna na `main` przed tym zadaniem: `/results` z `scenario_status: "Approved"` obok kosztu z żywego katalogu, what-if z hipotezą na migawce). W what-if koszt dodatkowy czytany przed strażnikiem i przed sprawdzeniem `draft`. Przychód niezależny od statusu (Story Points, Outcome-based) + zatwierdzenie między przychodem a kosztem = spójny wynik zatwierdzony (`/results` `200`, what-if `404`) — ważne, dopóki reguły tych modeli nie mają ścieżki edycji w wersji roboczej (R-06). Treść `409` generyczna niezależnie od uprawnień.
